## Summary

These contracts let a user act through an ECDSA signature and also by a direct call. 1,216 code families, 6,612 addresses, $868k held, 21,361 recoveries in the study's two-week window. Rows by rating: LOW 1,216. 51 rows were checked on a real transaction; the others were read from source, judged from bytecode or placed by measured rules (each row's note says which).

## Mechanism

PQ-19. A user whose account has real code loses the signed path only; gasless and relayed flows stop for that user.

## Evidence

- Each row's note says how it was decided: read from verified source, judged from bytecode and function names, placed by a rule measured against hand readings, or executed: a real transaction replayed on the fork's reference implementation with only the precompile changed, state at block 26,438,999.

- `0x4bacc118f9cee2f6e9b52aab317f8b5aaf2503f5` (unverified): $237k, LOW

Every row is in the header; `generated/triage_contracts.csv` lists them for search.

## Remediation

None needed for funds; add ERC-1271 to keep gasless flows.
